    3972              : 
    3973              : /// Garbage collection for lists and dictionaries.
    3974              : ///
    3975              : /// We use reference counts to be able to free most items right away when they
    3976              : /// are no longer used.  But for composite items it's possible that it becomes
    3977              : /// unused while the reference count is > 0: When there is a recursive
    3978              : /// reference.  Example:
    3979              : ///      :let l = [1, 2, 3]
    3980              : ///      :let d = {9: l}
    3981              : ///      :let l[1] = d
    3982              : ///
    3983              : /// Since this is quite unusual we handle this with garbage collection: every
    3984              : /// once in a while find out which lists and dicts are not referenced from any
